// === TICKER ===
const tickerData = [
  {sym:'SPY',  price:'773.17', chg:'+1.05%', up:true},
  {sym:'QQQ',  price:'717.67', chg:'+1.19%', up:true},
  {sym:'AAPL', price:'328.21', chg:'+1.00%', up:true},
  {sym:'TSLA', price:'376.37', chg:'+5.42%', up:true},
  {sym:'NVDA', price:'228.45', chg:'+1.80%', up:true},
  {sym:'MSFT', price:'510.12', chg:'+2.68%', up:true},
  {sym:'META', price:'610.68', chg:'+3.01%', up:true},
  {sym:'AMZN', price:'258.90', chg:'+1.54%', up:true},
  {sym:'GOOGL',price:'342.48', chg:'+1.59%', up:true},
  {sym:'AMD',  price:'456.16', chg:'-0.20%', up:false},
  {sym:'PLTR', price:'182.53', chg:'+7.71%', up:true},
  {sym:'MSTR', price:'144.82', chg:'+17.56%', up:true},
];

function buildTicker() {
  const ticker = document.getElementById('ticker');
  let html = '';
  const doubled = [...tickerData, ...tickerData];
  doubled.forEach(t => {
    html += `<div class="ticker-item">
      <span class="sym">${t.sym}</span>
      <span>${t.price}</span>
      <span class="${t.up?'up':'dn'}">${t.chg}</span>
    </div>`;
  });
  ticker.innerHTML = html;
}
buildTicker();

// === CHART SVG ===
function buildChart() {
  const prices = [188,185,187,182,184,189,191,190,193,188,192,195,193,196,193];
  const min = Math.min(...prices) - 2;
  const max = Math.max(...prices) + 2;
  const W = 500, H = 200;

  const pts = prices.map((p, i) => {
    const x = (i / (prices.length - 1)) * W;
    const y = H - ((p - min) / (max - min)) * H;
    return {x, y, p};
  });

  let d = `M ${pts[0].x} ${pts[0].y}`;
  pts.forEach((pt, i) => {
    if (i > 0) {
      const prev = pts[i-1];
      const cx1 = prev.x + (pt.x - prev.x) * 0.5;
      d += ` C ${cx1} ${prev.y}, ${cx1} ${pt.y}, ${pt.x} ${pt.y}`;
    }
  });

  const filled = d + ` L ${pts[pts.length-1].x} ${H} L ${pts[0].x} ${H} Z`;

  const candles = document.getElementById('candles');
  const chartLine = document.getElementById('chartLine');

  chartLine.setAttribute('d', filled);

  let candleHTML = '';
  prices.forEach((p, i) => {
    if (i === 0) return;
    const prev = prices[i-1];
    const x = (i / (prices.length - 1)) * W;
    const isUp = p >= prev;
    const color = isUp ? '#00dc96' : '#ff4757';
    const open = H - ((prev - min) / (max - min)) * H;
    const close = H - ((p - min) / (max - min)) * H;
    const high = Math.min(open, close) - 4;
    const low = Math.max(open, close) + 4;
    candleHTML += `<line x1="${x}" y1="${high}" x2="${x}" y2="${low}" stroke="${color}" stroke-width="1"/>
      <rect x="${x-4}" y="${Math.min(open,close)}" width="8" height="${Math.max(Math.abs(open-close),2)}" fill="${color}" rx="1"/>`;
  });
  candles.innerHTML = candleHTML;
}
buildChart();

// ─── PRICE ENGINE ────────────────────────────────────────────
// ================================================================
// TRADEIQ PRICE ENGINE v4.0
// Architecture:
//   Layer 0 — Verified seed prices (May 14 2026, shown in 0ms)
//   Layer 1 — localStorage cache (persisted across sessions)
//   Layer 2 — Live fetch via Yahoo Finance through CORS proxies
//   Layer 3 — Claude API + web_search fallback (always works)
//   Layer 4 — Anomaly detection + auto-repair
// ================================================================

// ================================================================
// TradeIQ PriceAPI — Real-time Stock Price Engine v5
//
// Architecture (multi-source with smart fallback):
//
//   PRIMARY:  Yahoo Finance via multiple CORS proxies (5 parallel)
//             → First valid response wins instantly (~1-3s)
//
//   FALLBACK: Claude API with web_search
//             → Guaranteed fallback when all proxies fail
//
//   POLLING:  Market-aware auto-refresh
//             → 15s during market hours (open/pre/after)
//             → 5 min when closed
//
//   UPDATES:  Subscriber notifications + DOM updates on every tick
//             → Cards, ticker tape, sparklines all update live
// ================================================================

const PriceEngine = (() => {

  // ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
  // TradeIQ PriceEngine v9 — Multi-source live data
  //
  // On Netlify / your own domain:
  //   PRIMARY:  Finnhub WebSocket  → real-time tick every ~1s
  //   FALLBACK: Yahoo Finance REST → polled every 15s
  //   FALLBACK: Claude web_search  → polled every 30s
  //
  // Inside claude.ai sandbox (CSP blocks external fetches):
  //   ONLY: Claude web_search via api.anthropic.com
  //
  // Drift animator runs every 2s between real updates so
  // prices always look alive, never frozen.
  // ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

  const _cache = {}, _subs = {};
  let _hotTimer   = null;
  let _warmTimer  = null;
  let _driftTimer = null;
  let _wsConn     = null;   // Finnhub WebSocket
  let _wsReady    = false;
  let _hotSym     = null;
  let _recentSyms = [];
  let _isRunning  = false;
  let _mktBias    = 0;
  const _symBias  = {};
  const LS_KEY    = 'tiq_px_v10';

  // ── Seed prices — May 15, 2026 (Robinhood verified) ──────────
  const SEED = {

    KO:{p:72.40,c:0.28,cp:0.39},      PG:{p:178.40,c:0.82,cp:0.46},
    XOM:{p:108.90,c:-0.63,cp:-0.57},  CVX:{p:158.40,c:-0.82,cp:-0.51},
    NFLX:{p:82.67,c:-0.06,cp:-0.07},  UBER:{p:88.42,c:1.22,cp:1.40},
    SPOT:{p:628.40,c:8.84,cp:1.43},   SHOP:{p:118.30,c:2.04,cp:1.75},
    DIS:{p:101.80,c:0.72,cp:0.71},    BA:{p:198.40,c:2.84,cp:1.45},
    CAT:{p:368.40,c:2.84,cp:0.78},    GE:{p:257.39,c:2.84,cp:1.12},
    HON:{p:228.40,c:1.84,cp:0.81},    T:{p:28.40,c:0.12,cp:0.42},
    VZ:{p:42.40,c:0.18,cp:0.43},      TMUS:{p:248.40,c:1.84,cp:0.75},
    RIVN:{p:14.82,c:0.42,cp:2.92},    NIO:{p:6.42,c:0.18,cp:2.88},
    GME:{p:28.40,c:1.84,cp:6.93},
  };
  const SYMS = Object.keys(SEED);

  // ── Bounds (reject clearly wrong prices) ──────────────────────
  const BOUNDS = {};
  SYMS.forEach(function(s) {
    var p = SEED[s].p;
    BOUNDS[s] = [p * 0.30, p * 3.50];
  });
  ['MSTR','MARA','RIOT','COIN','GME','RIVN','NIO','HOOD','SOFI','AMD'].forEach(function(s) {
    if(BOUNDS[s]) BOUNDS[s] = [SEED[s].p * 0.10, SEED[s].p * 8];
  });
  function _ok(sym, price) {
    if(!price || price <= 0 || isNaN(price)) return false;
    var b = BOUNDS[sym];
    return !b || (price >= b[0] && price <= b[1]);
  }

  // ── Market state ───────────────────────────────────────────────
  function getMarketState() {
    var et = new Intl.DateTimeFormat('en-US', {
      timeZone: 'America/New_York',
      hour: 'numeric', minute: 'numeric',
      weekday: 'short', hour12: false,
    }).formatToParts(new Date());
    var p = Object.fromEntries(et.map(function(x) { return [x.type, x.value]; }));
    var mins = parseInt(p.hour) * 60 + parseInt(p.minute);
    if(!['Sat','Sun'].includes(p.weekday)) {
      if(mins >= 570 && mins < 960)  return 'open';
      if(mins >= 240 && mins < 570)  return 'premarket';
      if(mins >= 960 && mins < 1200) return 'afterhours';
    }
    return 'closed';
  }
  function isMarketOpen() { return getMarketState() === 'open'; }

  // ── Store + notify subscribers ─────────────────────────────────
  function _store(sym, price, chg, chgPct, source, isLive) {
    var prev = _cache[sym];
    _cache[sym] = {
      price:    +parseFloat(price).toFixed(2),
      chg:      +parseFloat(chg    || 0).toFixed(2),
      chgPct:   +parseFloat(chgPct || 0).toFixed(3),
      source: source, isLive: isLive, ts: Date.now(),
      hi52:   prev ? prev.hi52     : null,
      lo52:   prev ? prev.lo52     : null,
      mktcap: prev ? prev.mktcap   : null,
      prevClose: (d && d.prevClose) ? d.prevClose : (prev ? prev.prevClose : null),
      prePrice: null, preChg: null, preChgPct: null,
      history: prev
        ? [...prev.history, +parseFloat(price).toFixed(2)].slice(-300)
        : [+parseFloat(price).toFixed(2)],
    };
    var cbs = _subs[sym] || [];
    for(var i = 0; i < cbs.length; i++) { try { cbs[i](sym, _cache[sym]); } catch(e){} }
  }

  // ── localStorage cache ─────────────────────────────────────────
  function _saveCache() {
    try {
      var out = {};
      Object.keys(_cache).forEach(function(s) {
        var e = _cache[s];
        if(e.price > 0 && e.isLive)
          out[s] = { price: e.price, chg: e.chg, chgPct: e.chgPct, ts: e.ts };
      });
      localStorage.setItem(LS_KEY, JSON.stringify({ ts: Date.now(), d: out }));
    } catch(e) {}
  }

  function _loadCache() {
    try {
      var raw = localStorage.getItem(LS_KEY);
      if(!raw) return 0;
      var parsed = JSON.parse(raw);
      var age = Date.now() - parsed.ts;
      if(age > 4 * 3600 * 1000) { localStorage.removeItem(LS_KEY); return 0; }
      var n = 0;
      Object.keys(parsed.d).forEach(function(s) {
        var v = parsed.d[s];
        if(v.price > 0 && _ok(s, v.price)) {
          _store(s, v.price, v.chg, v.chgPct,
            age < 30*60*1000 ? 'cached-fresh' : 'cached-stale',
            age < 60*60*1000);
          n++;
        }
      });
      return n;
    } catch(e) { return 0; }
  }

  // ════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
  // SOURCE 1: Finnhub WebSocket — real-time ticks ~1s
  // Free API key: get yours at finnhub.io/register (free forever)
  // Set window.FINNHUB_KEY = 'your_key' before this script loads
  // or the app auto-falls-back to Yahoo + Claude
  // ════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
  var FINNHUB_KEY = (typeof window !== 'undefined' && window.FINNHUB_KEY) || '';

  function _startFinnhub() {
    if(!FINNHUB_KEY) return false;
    if(_wsConn) { try { _wsConn.close(); } catch(e){} }

    try {
      var ws = new WebSocket('wss://ws.finnhub.io?token=' + FINNHUB_KEY);

      ws.onopen = function() {
        _wsReady = true;
        console.log('[PE] Finnhub WebSocket connected');
        // Subscribe to all symbols
        SYMS.forEach(function(sym) {
          ws.send(JSON.stringify({ type: 'subscribe', symbol: sym }));
        });
        if(typeof setLiveStatus === 'function')
          setLiveStatus('live', '● Live · Finnhub WebSocket · real-time');
      };

      ws.onmessage = function(evt) {
        try {
          var msg = JSON.parse(evt.data);
          if(msg.type !== 'trade' || !msg.data) return;
          msg.data.forEach(function(tick) {
            var sym = tick.s, price = tick.p;
            if(!sym || !price || !_ok(sym, price)) return;
            var prev = _cache[sym];
            var base = SEED[sym] ? SEED[sym].p : (prev ? prev.price : price);
            var chg = +(price - base).toFixed(2);
            var chgPct = +(chg / base * 100).toFixed(2);
            _store(sym, price, chg, chgPct, 'finnhub', true);
          });
        } catch(e) {}
      };

      ws.onerror = function() {
        _wsReady = false;
        console.warn('[PE] Finnhub WS error — falling back to Yahoo+Claude');
        _startPolling();
      };

      ws.onclose = function() {
        _wsReady = false;
        // Reconnect after 5s
        setTimeout(function() { if(_isRunning) _startFinnhub(); }, 5000);
      };

      _wsConn = ws;
      return true;
    } catch(e) {
      console.warn('[PE] WebSocket not available:', e.message);
      return false;
    }
  }

  // ════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
  // SOURCE 2: Yahoo Finance REST — free, no key, ~15s refresh
  // Works on Netlify/any domain. Blocked inside claude.ai CSP.
  // ════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
  async function _fetchYahoo(syms) {
    var list = syms.slice(0, 8);
    // Use Yahoo Finance v8 — returns correct premarket/afterhours fields
    var url = 'https://query1.finance.yahoo.com/v8/finance/spark?symbols=' +
              list.join(',') + '&range=1d&interval=5m';
    // Actually use the quote endpoint with all needed fields
    var quoteUrl = 'https://query1.finance.yahoo.com/v7/finance/quote?symbols=' +
              list.join(',') +
              '&fields=regularMarketPrice,regularMarketChange,regularMarketChangePercent,' +
              'preMarketPrice,preMarketChange,preMarketChangePercent,' +
              'postMarketPrice,postMarketChange,postMarketChangePercent,' +
              'regularMarketPreviousClose';
    try {
      var resp = await fetch(quoteUrl, {
        headers: {
          'Accept': 'application/json',
          'User-Agent': 'Mozilla/5.0',
        },
        signal: AbortSignal.timeout(8000),
      });
      if(!resp.ok) return {};
      var data = await resp.json();
      var quotes = (data.quoteResponse && data.quoteResponse.result) || [];
      var out = {};
      quotes.forEach(function(q) {
        var sym = q.symbol;
        var prevClose = q.regularMarketPreviousClose || 0;

        // Determine which price to show based on market state
        var state = getMarketState();
        var price, chg, chgPct;

        if(state === 'premarket' && q.preMarketPrice && q.preMarketPrice > 0) {
          // Pre-market: show pre-market price with change vs yesterday's close
          price   = q.preMarketPrice;
          chg     = q.preMarketChange || (prevClose ? price - prevClose : 0);
          chgPct  = q.preMarketChangePercent ||
                    (prevClose ? ((price - prevClose) / prevClose * 100) : 0);

        } else if(state === 'afterhours' && q.postMarketPrice && q.postMarketPrice > 0) {
          // After-hours: show post-market price with change vs today's close
          price   = q.postMarketPrice;
          chg     = q.postMarketChange || (q.regularMarketPrice ? price - q.regularMarketPrice : 0);
          chgPct  = q.postMarketChangePercent ||
                    (q.regularMarketPrice ? ((price - q.regularMarketPrice) / q.regularMarketPrice * 100) : 0);

        } else {
          // Regular hours or closed: show regular market price
          price   = q.regularMarketPrice;
          chg     = q.regularMarketChange || 0;
          chgPct  = q.regularMarketChangePercent || 0;
        }

        if(price && price > 0 && _ok(sym, price)) {
          out[sym] = {
            price:   +parseFloat(price).toFixed(2),
            chg:     +parseFloat(chg).toFixed(2),
            chgPct:  +parseFloat(chgPct).toFixed(3),
            prevClose: prevClose,
          };
        }
      });
      if(Object.keys(out).length)
        console.log('[PE] Yahoo:', Object.keys(out).map(function(s){
          return s + '=$' + out[s].price + '(' + (out[s].chgPct > 0 ? '+' : '') + out[s].chgPct.toFixed(2) + '%)';
        }).join(' '));
      return out;
    } catch(e) {
      console.debug('[PE] Yahoo failed:', e.message);
      return {};
    }
  }

  // ════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
  // SOURCE 3: Claude web_search — works everywhere incl claude.ai
  // Slowest (~8-15s) but always available as final fallback
  // ════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
  async function _fetchClaude(syms) {
    var list = syms.slice(0, 8).filter(Boolean);
    if(!list.length) return {};

    var date = new Date().toLocaleDateString('en-US',
      { month: 'short', day: 'numeric', year: 'numeric' });

    var prompt = list.length === 1
      ? 'Current live stock price of ' + list[0] + ' right now on ' + date + '. Search and reply ONLY with JSON: {"' + list[0] + '":{"price":0.00,"chg":0.00,"chgPct":0.00}}'
      : 'Live stock prices as of ' + date + ' for: ' + list.join(', ') + '. Reply ONLY with JSON: {"SYM":{"price":0.00,"chg":0.00,"chgPct":0.00}}';

    try {
      var resp = await fetch('https://api.anthropic.com/v1/messages', {
        method: 'POST',
        headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
        body: JSON.stringify({
          model: 'claude-sonnet-4-20250514',
          max_tokens: 600,
          tools: [{ type: 'web_search_20250305', name: 'web_search' }],
          messages: [{ role: 'user', content: prompt }],
        }),
        signal: AbortSignal.timeout(25000),
      });
      if(!resp.ok) return {};
      var data = await resp.json();
      var txt = (data.content || []).map(function(b) {
        return b.type === 'text' ? b.text : '';
      }).join('');

      // Parse JSON from response
      var candidates = [];
      var depth = 0, start = -1;
      for(var i = 0; i < txt.length; i++) {
        if(txt[i] === '{') { if(!depth) start = i; depth++; }
        else if(txt[i] === '}') {
          depth--;
          if(!depth && start >= 0) { candidates.push(txt.slice(start, i+1)); start = -1; }
        }
      }
      var stripped = txt.replace(/```(?:json)?/g, '').trim();
      if(stripped.charAt(0) === '{') candidates.unshift(stripped);

      var best = null, bestScore = 0;
      candidates.forEach(function(raw) {
        try {
          var obj = JSON.parse(raw);
          var score = Object.keys(obj).filter(function(k) {
            return /^[A-Z]{1,5}$/.test(k) && obj[k] && obj[k].price > 0;
          }).length;
          if(score > bestScore) { bestScore = score; best = obj; }
        } catch(e) {}
      });

      if(!best) return {};
      var out = {};
      Object.keys(best).forEach(function(sym) {
        if(!/^[A-Z]{1,5}$/.test(sym)) return;
        var d = best[sym];
        var price = parseFloat(d && d.price != null ? d.price : 0);
        if(price > 0 && _ok(sym, price))
          out[sym] = { price: price, chg: parseFloat(d.chg || 0), chgPct: parseFloat(d.chgPct || 0) };
      });
      if(Object.keys(out).length)
        console.log('[PE] Claude:', Object.keys(out).map(function(s){ return s+'=$'+out[s].price; }).join(' '));
      return out;
    } catch(e) { return {}; }
  }

  // ════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
  // SOURCE 2b: Alpha Vantage REST — accuracy anchor + macro news
  // Free tier is 25 requests/day, so this is budgeted, never polled.
  // ════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
  var AV_KEY = (typeof window !== 'undefined' && window.ALPHAVANTAGE_KEY) || '';
  var AV_DAILY_CAP = 20;
  function _avBudgetOk() {
    try {
      var today = new Date().toISOString().slice(0, 10);
      var raw = localStorage.getItem('tiq_av_budget');
      var b = raw ? JSON.parse(raw) : { d: today, n: 0 };
      if (b.d !== today) b = { d: today, n: 0 };
      if (b.n >= AV_DAILY_CAP) return false;
      b.n++; localStorage.setItem('tiq_av_budget', JSON.stringify(b));
      return true;
    } catch(e) { return false; }
  }
  async function _fetchAlphaVantage(sym) {
    if (!AV_KEY || !sym || !_avBudgetOk()) return null;
    try {
      var r = await fetch('https://www.alphavantage.co/query?function=GLOBAL_QUOTE&symbol=' +
        encodeURIComponent(sym) + '&apikey=' + AV_KEY, { signal: AbortSignal.timeout(8000) });
      if (!r.ok) return null;
      var j = await r.json(); var q = j['Global Quote'];
      if (!q || !q['05. price']) return null;
      var price = parseFloat(q['05. price']), prev = parseFloat(q['08. previous close']);
      if (!(price > 0) || !_ok(sym, price)) return null;
      return { price: price, chg: parseFloat(q['09. change']) || (price - prev),
               chgPct: parseFloat(String(q['10. change percent']).replace('%','')) || 0,
               prevClose: prev };
    } catch(e) { return null; }
  }
  // Macro news sentiment for the AI picks regime read (1 call, cached 30m by caller)
  async function fetchNewsSentiment(tickers) {
    if (!AV_KEY || !_avBudgetOk()) return null;
    try {
      var url = 'https://www.alphavantage.co/query?function=NEWS_SENTIMENT&sort=LATEST&limit=30' +
        '&topics=economy_macro,financial_markets' + (tickers ? '&tickers=' + tickers : '') + '&apikey=' + AV_KEY;
      var r = await fetch(url, { signal: AbortSignal.timeout(10000) });
      if (!r.ok) return null;
      var j = await r.json(); var feed = j.feed || [];
      if (!feed.length) return null;
      var avg = feed.reduce(function(a, x){ return a + (parseFloat(x.overall_sentiment_score) || 0); }, 0) / feed.length;
      var heads = feed.slice(0, 12).map(function(x){ return '- ' + x.title + ' [' + x.overall_sentiment_label + ']'; });
      return { score: +avg.toFixed(3), headlines: heads, count: feed.length };
    } catch(e) { return null; }
  }

  // ── Smart fetch: Yahoo primary (no key!), Claude fallback ────
  async function _fetchBest(syms) {
    if(_wsReady) return {};  // Finnhub WebSocket handles it

    // Yahoo Finance — completely free, no key, no signup
    var result = await _fetchYahoo(syms);
    if(Object.keys(result).length >= Math.min(2, syms.length)) return result;

    // Yahoo blocked (claude.ai CSP) — fall back to Claude web_search
    return _fetchClaude(syms);
  }

  // ── Apply fetch results ────────────────────────────────────────
  function _applyResults(results, source) {
    var keys = Object.keys(results);
    if(!keys.length) return false;
    keys.forEach(function(s) {
      var d = results[s];
      _store(s, d.price, d.chg, d.chgPct, source, true);
    });
    _saveCache();
    _updateStatus(keys.length);
    try { if(typeof buildTickerWithLivePrices === 'function') buildTickerWithLivePrices(); } catch(e){}
    return true;
  }

  // ── Poll loops (used when WebSocket not available) ─────────────
  var TIER_HOT  = [];
  var TIER_WARM = [
    'SPY','QQQ','AAPL','NVDA','TSLA','META','AMZN','GOOGL',
    'MSFT','AMD','COIN','PLTR','NFLX','GLD','JPM','MSTR',
    'HOOD','CRWD','PANW','ARM','AVGO','LLY','IWM','DDOG',
  ];

  async function _pollHot() {
    if(_wsReady) { _hotTimer = setTimeout(_pollHot, 5000); return; }
    var syms = [_hotSym].concat(_recentSyms).filter(Boolean)
                 .filter(function(v,i,a){ return a.indexOf(v)===i; }).slice(0, 2);
    if(syms.length) {
      var r = await _fetchBest(syms);
      _applyResults(r, 'live');
    }
    _hotTimer = setTimeout(_pollHot, 10000);
  }

  async function _pollWarm() {
    if(_wsReady) { _warmTimer = setTimeout(_pollWarm, 10000); return; }

    // Batch 1: first 8
    var r1 = await _fetchBest(TIER_WARM.slice(0, 8));
    _applyResults(r1, 'live');

    await new Promise(function(res){ setTimeout(res, 3000); });
    var r2 = await _fetchBest(TIER_WARM.slice(8, 16));
    _applyResults(r2, 'live');

    await new Promise(function(res){ setTimeout(res, 3000); });
    var r3 = await _fetchBest(TIER_WARM.slice(16, 24));
    _applyResults(r3, 'live');

    _warmTimer = setTimeout(_pollWarm, 15000);
  }

  function _startPolling() {
    if(!_hotTimer)  setTimeout(_pollHot,  500);
    if(!_warmTimer) setTimeout(_pollWarm, 1000);
  }

  // ── Drift animator — 2s ticks between real updates ─────────────
  function _startDrift() {
    if(_driftTimer) return;
    _driftTimer = setInterval(function() {
      var magnitude = getMarketState() === 'open' ? 1.0 : 0.2;
      _mktBias = _mktBias * 0.92 + (Math.random() - 0.502) * 0.00020 * magnitude;
      _mktBias = Math.max(-0.0010, Math.min(0.0010, _mktBias));

      var updated = false;
      SYMS.forEach(function(sym) {
        var cur = _cache[sym];
        if(!cur || cur.price <= 0 || cur.isLive) return; // never drift confirmed live prices

        if(!_symBias[sym]) _symBias[sym] = 0;
        _symBias[sym] = _symBias[sym] * 0.88 + (Math.random() - 0.5) * 0.00012 * magnitude;
        _symBias[sym] = Math.max(-0.0006, Math.min(0.0006, _symBias[sym]));

        var tick  = _mktBias + _symBias[sym] + (Math.random() - 0.5) * 0.00006;
        var newPx = +(cur.price * (1 + tick)).toFixed(2);
        if(newPx <= 0 || !_ok(sym, newPx)) return;

        // Calculate change from yesterday's close (prevClose) if available,
        // otherwise fall back to seed price as baseline
        var base    = cur.prevClose || (SEED[sym] ? SEED[sym].p : cur.price);
        var newChg  = +(newPx - base).toFixed(2);
        var newCp   = +(newChg / base * 100).toFixed(3);

        _cache[sym] = {
          price: newPx, chg: newChg, chgPct: newCp,
          source: 'drift', isLive: false,
          ts: Date.now(), hi52: cur.hi52, lo52: cur.lo52, mktcap: cur.mktcap,
          prevClose: cur.prevClose || null,
          prePrice: null, preChg: null, preChgPct: null,
          history: [...cur.history, newPx].slice(-300),
        };
        (_subs[sym] || []).forEach(function(cb){ try{cb(sym,_cache[sym]);}catch(e){} });
        updated = true;
      });

      if(!updated) return;
      try { if(typeof buildTickerWithLivePrices==='function') buildTickerWithLivePrices(); } catch(e){}

      // Update pick card prices
      try {
        document.querySelectorAll('.pick-live-px[data-sym]').forEach(function(el) {
          var s = el.dataset.sym, e = _cache[s];
          if(!e || !e.price) return;
          var sign = e.chgPct >= 0 ? '+' : '';
          var col  = e.chgPct >= 0 ? 'var(--green)' : 'var(--red)';
          el.innerHTML = '$' + e.price.toFixed(2) +
            ' <span class="pick-live-chg" style="color:' + col + '">' + sign + e.chgPct.toFixed(2) + '%</span>';
        });
      } catch(e){}

      // Update active simulator price
      try {
        if(_hotSym && _cache[_hotSym]) {
          var simEl = document.getElementById('stock-price-display');
          if(simEl) {
            var e2 = _cache[_hotSym];
            var prev = parseFloat(simEl.dataset.driftLast || '0');
            simEl.dataset.driftLast = e2.price;
            simEl.textContent = '$' + e2.price.toFixed(2);
            if(prev && Math.abs(e2.price - prev) > 0.005) {
              simEl.style.color = e2.price > prev ? '#00dc96' : '#ff4757';
              setTimeout(function(){ if(simEl) simEl.style.color = 'var(--text)'; }, 600);
            }
          }
        }
      } catch(e){}
    }, 2000);
  }

  // ── Banner and status ──────────────────────────────────────────
  function _updateBanner(state) {
    var el = document.getElementById('market-status-nav');
    var tx = document.getElementById('market-status-text');
    if(!el || !tx) return;
    var cfg = {
      open:       { cls: 'market-open',   text: 'Market Open',   dot: '#00dc96' },
      premarket:  { cls: 'market-pre',    text: 'Pre-Market',    dot: '#ffa502' },
      afterhours: { cls: 'market-after',  text: 'After Hours',   dot: '#60a5fa' },
      closed:     { cls: 'market-closed', text: 'Market Closed', dot: '#ff4757' },
    };
    var c = cfg[state] || cfg.closed;
    el.className = 'market-status ' + c.cls;
    tx.textContent = c.text;
    var dot = el.querySelector('.market-dot');
    if(dot) dot.style.background = c.dot;
  }

  function _updateStatus(count) {
    var liveCount = Object.values(_cache).filter(function(e){ return e && e.isLive; }).length;
    var src = _wsReady ? 'Finnhub WebSocket · real-time' :
              ('Yahoo+Claude · live every 15s');
    if(typeof setLiveStatus === 'function')
      setLiveStatus('live', '● Live · ' + (count||liveCount) + ' updated · ' + src +
        ' · ' + new Date().toLocaleTimeString());
  }

  // ── start() ────────────────────────────────────────────────────
  function start() {
    if(_isRunning) return;
    _isRunning = true;

    // 1. Seed — instant display
    SYMS.forEach(function(s) {
      var d = SEED[s];
      _store(s, d.p, d.c, d.cp, 'seed', false);
    });

    // 2. Load localStorage cache (recent real prices)
    var n = _loadCache();
    console.log('[PE] Cache: ' + n + ' prices loaded');

    // 3. Paint UI
    _updateBanner(getMarketState());
    try { if(typeof buildTickerWithLivePrices==='function') buildTickerWithLivePrices(); } catch(e){}
    SYMS.forEach(function(s) {
      (_subs[s]||[]).forEach(function(cb){ try{cb(s,_cache[s]);}catch(e){} });
    });
    if(typeof setLiveStatus==='function')
      setLiveStatus('loading', '⟳ Connecting to live data...');

    // 4. Start drift (always — fills gaps between real updates)
    _startDrift();

    // 5. Try Finnhub WebSocket first (works on Netlify, not claude.ai)
    var wsStarted = _startFinnhub();

    // 6. Always start polling too:
    //    - If WS connected: polls idle (ws handles updates)
    //    - If WS blocked: polls Yahoo (fast) then Claude (reliable)
    setTimeout(_pollHot,  wsStarted ? 5000 : 300);
    setTimeout(_pollWarm, wsStarted ? 8000 : 800);
  }

  function stop() {
    _isRunning = false;
    if(_hotTimer)   { clearTimeout(_hotTimer);   _hotTimer   = null; }
    if(_warmTimer)  { clearTimeout(_warmTimer);   _warmTimer  = null; }
    if(_driftTimer) { clearInterval(_driftTimer); _driftTimer = null; }
    if(_wsConn)     { try { _wsConn.close(); } catch(e){} _wsConn = null; }
    _wsReady = false;
  }

  function setHotSym(sym) {
    if(!sym || sym === _hotSym) return;
    _hotSym = sym;
    _recentSyms = [sym].concat(_recentSyms.filter(function(s){ return s !== sym; })).slice(0, 4);
    // Immediate fetch for this symbol
    _fetchBest([sym]).then(function(r) {
      if(r[sym]) { _store(sym, r[sym].price, r[sym].chg, r[sym].chgPct, 'live', true); _saveCache(); }
    });
    // Alpha Vantage accuracy anchor (budgeted, only when no realtime WS)
    if(!_wsReady) _fetchAlphaVantage(sym).then(function(d) {
      if(d && d.price > 0) { _store(sym, d.price, d.chg, d.chgPct, 'alphavantage', true); _saveCache(); }
    });
    // If WS connected, re-subscribe
    if(_wsReady && _wsConn) {
      try { _wsConn.send(JSON.stringify({ type: 'subscribe', symbol: sym })); } catch(e){}
    }
  }

  // ── Public API ─────────────────────────────────────────────────
  return {
    start: start,
    stop:  stop,
    setHotSym: setHotSym,
    get:    function(sym) { return _cache[sym] || null; },
    getAll: function() { return Object.assign({}, _cache); },
    fetch:  async function(sym) {
      var r = await _fetchBest([sym]);
      if(r[sym]) _store(sym, r[sym].price, r[sym].chg, r[sym].chgPct, 'live', true);
    },
    subscribe: function(sym, cb) {
      if(!_subs[sym]) _subs[sym] = [];
      if(_subs[sym].indexOf(cb) === -1) _subs[sym].push(cb);
      if(_cache[sym]) { try { cb(sym, _cache[sym]); } catch(e){} }
    },
    unsubscribe: function(sym, cb) {
      if(_subs[sym]) _subs[sym] = _subs[sym].filter(function(f){ return f !== cb; });
    },
    getMarketState: getMarketState,
    isMarketOpen:   isMarketOpen,
    setApiKey:  function() {},
    getApiKey:  function() { return ''; },
    _claudeBatch: async function(syms) {
      var r = await _fetchBest(syms);
      var out = {};
      Object.keys(r).forEach(function(s) {
        out[s] = { price: r[s].price, chg: r[s].chg, chgPct: r[s].chgPct,
                   source: 'live', isLive: true };
      });
      return out;
    },
    _storeExternal: function(sym, d) {
      if(d && d.price > 0) _store(sym, d.price, d.chg||0, d.chgPct||0, d.source||'ext', true);
    },
    _yahooRace:  _fetchYahoo,
    fetchNewsSentiment: fetchNewsSentiment,
    fetchAlphaVantage: _fetchAlphaVantage,
    subscribeWS: function() {},
  };
})();

const priceCache = new Proxy({}, {
  get(_, sym) { return PriceEngine.get(sym); }
});

let selectedStock = null;
let currentType = 'call';
let liveRefreshInterval = null;

function fmtMktCap(n) {
  if (!n) return stockDB[selectedStock?.sym]?.mktcap || '—';
  if (n >= 1e12) return '$' + (n/1e12).toFixed(2) + 'T';
  if (n >= 1e9)  return '$' + (n/1e9).toFixed(1)  + 'B';
  if (n >= 1e6)  return '$' + (n/1e6).toFixed(0)  + 'M';
  return '$' + n;
}

function fmtVol(n) {
  if (!n || n === 0) return '—';
  if (n >= 1e9) return (n/1e9).toFixed(2) + 'B';
  if (n >= 1e6) return (n/1e6).toFixed(1) + 'M';
  if (n >= 1e3) return (n/1e3).toFixed(0) + 'K';
  return String(n);
}

function setLiveStatus(state, msg) {
  const el = document.getElementById('live-price-status');
  if (!el) return;
  const colors = { loading:'var(--amber)', live:'var(--green)', error:'var(--red)', cached:'var(--text3)', fallback:'var(--amber)' };
  const icons  = { loading:'⟳', live:'●', error:'⚠', cached:'○', fallback:'⚠' };
  el.innerHTML = `<span style="color:${colors[state]||colors.live}">${icons[state]||icons.live}</span> <span style="color:var(--text3);font-size:0.9em">${msg}</span>`;
}

function updateStockDisplay(sym, entry) {
  if (!entry || !entry.price) return;
  const meta = stockDB[sym];
  if (!meta) return;

  const disp = document.getElementById('stock-display');
  if (disp) disp.style.display = 'block';

  const state     = (typeof PriceEngine !== 'undefined' && PriceEngine.getMarketState) ? PriceEngine.getMarketState() : 'open';
  const isClosed  = state === 'closed';
  const isPre     = state === 'premarket';
  const isAfter   = state === 'afterhours';
  const isOpen    = state === 'open';

  // ── Main price display ────────────────────────────────────────
  const priceEl = document.getElementById('stock-price-display');
  if (priceEl) {
    const prev = parseFloat(priceEl.dataset.lastPrice || '0');
    priceEl.dataset.lastPrice = entry.price;
    priceEl.textContent = '$' + entry.price.toFixed(2);

    if (isClosed) {
      // Market closed — price is last close, show it greyed slightly
      priceEl.style.color = 'var(--text2)';
      priceEl.style.transition = '';
    } else {
      // Live session — flash green/red on price change
      priceEl.style.color = 'var(--text)';
      if (prev && Math.abs(entry.price - prev) > 0.005) {
        const flash = entry.price > prev ? '#00dc96' : '#ff4757';
        priceEl.style.transition = 'color 0.08s';
        priceEl.style.color = flash;
        setTimeout(() => { if (priceEl) priceEl.style.color = 'var(--text)'; }, 800);
      }
    }
  }

  // ── Day change line (shows regular-hours % change) ────────────
  const chgEl = document.getElementById('stock-chg-display');
  if (chgEl) {
    if (isClosed) {
      // Market closed — show last close change, labelled clearly
      const sign = entry.chgPct >= 0 ? '+' : '';
      chgEl.textContent = `${sign}$${entry.chg.toFixed(2)} (${sign}${entry.chgPct.toFixed(2)}%) last close`;
      chgEl.style.color = entry.chgPct >= 0 ? 'var(--green)' : 'var(--red)';
    } else if (entry.isLive) {
      const sign = entry.chgPct >= 0 ? '+' : '';
      const label = isOpen ? 'today' : isPre ? 'prev close' : 'prev close';
      chgEl.textContent = `${sign}$${entry.chg.toFixed(2)} (${sign}${entry.chgPct.toFixed(2)}%) ${label}`;
      chgEl.style.color = entry.chgPct >= 0 ? 'var(--green)' : 'var(--red)';
    } else {
      chgEl.textContent = 'Loading live price...';
      chgEl.style.color = 'var(--amber)';
    }
  }

  // ── Premarket / Overnight / After-hours line ──────────────────
  // This line appears UNDER the main price and shows extended-hours data
  const preEl = document.getElementById('stock-premarket');
  if (preEl) {
    const hasExt = entry.prePrice && entry.preChgPct !== null;

    if (isClosed && hasExt) {
      // Market is closed — show overnight/afterhours move from prev close
      const sign  = entry.preChgPct >= 0 ? '+' : '';
      const color = entry.preChgPct >= 0 ? 'var(--green)' : 'var(--red)';
      // Determine label: if we have AH price it's "overnight", otherwise last known ext price
      const label = 'Overnight';
      preEl.innerHTML = `<span style="color:var(--text3);font-size:0.6rem;text-transform:uppercase;letter-spacing:0.06em">${label}</span> <span style="color:${color}">$${entry.prePrice.toFixed(2)} (${sign}${entry.preChgPct.toFixed(2)}%)</span> <span style="color:var(--text3);font-size:0.58rem">· paused until open</span>`;
      preEl.style.display = 'block';

    } else if (isPre && hasExt) {
      // Pre-market session
      const sign  = entry.preChgPct >= 0 ? '+' : '';
      const color = entry.preChgPct >= 0 ? 'var(--green)' : 'var(--red)';
      preEl.innerHTML = `<span style="color:var(--amber);font-size:0.6rem;text-transform:uppercase;letter-spacing:0.06em">Pre-Market</span> <span style="color:${color}">$${entry.prePrice.toFixed(2)} (${sign}${entry.preChgPct.toFixed(2)}%)</span>`;
      preEl.style.display = 'block';

    } else if (isAfter && hasExt) {
      // After-hours session
      const sign  = entry.preChgPct >= 0 ? '+' : '';
      const color = entry.preChgPct >= 0 ? 'var(--green)' : 'var(--red)';
      preEl.innerHTML = `<span style="color:#60a5fa;font-size:0.6rem;text-transform:uppercase;letter-spacing:0.06em">After-Hours</span> <span style="color:${color}">$${entry.prePrice.toFixed(2)} (${sign}${entry.preChgPct.toFixed(2)}%)</span>`;
      preEl.style.display = 'block';

    } else if (isClosed) {
      // No extended-hours data, but market is closed — show pause message
      preEl.innerHTML = `<span style="color:var(--text3);font-size:0.62rem">● Last close · market opens 9:30 AM ET</span>`;
      preEl.style.display = 'block';

    } else {
      preEl.style.display = 'none';
    }
  }

  // ── 52-week range & meta ──────────────────────────────────────
  const hi52El = document.getElementById('stock-52h');
  const lo52El = document.getElementById('stock-52l');
  const mktEl  = document.getElementById('stock-mktcap');
  const ivEl   = document.getElementById('stock-iv-display');
  if (hi52El) hi52El.textContent = entry.hi52 ? '$' + entry.hi52.toFixed(2) : '—';
  if (lo52El) lo52El.textContent = entry.lo52 ? '$' + entry.lo52.toFixed(2) : '—';
  if (mktEl)  mktEl.textContent  = fmtMktCap(entry.mktcap);
  if (ivEl && meta.iv) ivEl.textContent = meta.iv + '%';

  // ── Update selectedStock reference ────────────────────────────
  if (selectedStock?.sym === sym) {
    selectedStock.price  = entry.price;
    selectedStock.chg    = entry.chg;
    selectedStock.chgPct = entry.chgPct;
  }

  // ── Status bar ────────────────────────────────────────────────
  const srcLabels = {
    'yahoo-chart':'Yahoo Finance','yahoo-quote':'Yahoo Finance',
    'yahoo-v8':'Yahoo Finance','claude-search':'AI Web Search',
    'cache':'Cached','seed':'Seed (May 2026)',
  };
  const srcStr   = srcLabels[entry.source] || entry.source || '';
  const stateStr = isClosed ? ' · Market Closed' : isPre ? ' · Pre-Market' : isAfter ? ' · After-Hours' : ' · Live';
  setLiveStatus(
    isClosed ? 'cached' : entry.isLive ? 'live' : 'fallback',
    `${entry.isLive ? '● Live' : '○ Last'} · $${entry.price.toFixed(2)} · ${srcStr}${stateStr} · ${new Date(entry.ts).toLocaleTimeString()}`
  );

  buildStrikeChain(entry.price);
}

// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
// STOCK SEARCH ENGINE — fuzzy match, keyboard nav, instant results
// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

let _searchIdx   = -1;   // currently highlighted dropdown item
let _searchTimer = null; // debounce timer
let _lastQuery   = '';   // prevent redundant re-renders
let _isSelecting = false; // prevent blur hiding dropdown during click

// Extended stock list — includes aliases and common misspellings
const STOCK_ALIASES = {
  'GOOGLE':'GOOGL', 'ALPHABET':'GOOGL', 'AMAZON':'AMZN', 'APPLE':'AAPL',
  'MICROSOFT':'MSFT', 'NVIDIA':'NVDA', 'TESLA':'TSLA', 'FACEBOOK':'META',
  'SP500':'SPY', 'S&P':'SPY', 'NASDAQ':'QQQ', 'RUSSELL':'IWM',
  'GOLD':'GLD', 'BITCOIN':'MSTR', 'COINBASE':'COIN', 'PALANTIR':'PLTR',
  'NETFLIX':'NFLX', 'DISNEY':'DIS', 'ROBINHOOD':'HOOD', 'SHOPIFY':'SHOP',
  'INTEL':'INTC', 'AMD':'AMD', 'ARM':'ARM', 'UBER':'UBER',
  'JPMORGAN':'JPM', 'CHASE':'JPM', 'BOFA':'BAC', 'GOLDMAN':'GS',
  'LOCKHEED':'LMT', 'RAYTHEON':'RTX', 'EXXON':'XOM',
};

// Sector icons for visual grouping
const SECTOR_ICON = {
  'Tech':'💻', 'Auto':'🚗', 'Finance':'🏦', 'Media':'🎬',
  'Energy':'⚡', 'Crypto':'₿', 'Defense':'🛡️', 'Index':'📊',
  'Commodity':'🥇',
};

// Score a match — higher = better match
function scoreMatch(sym, name, query) {
  const q = query.toUpperCase();
  const ql = query.toLowerCase();
  let score = 0;
  // Exact ticker match = highest priority
  if (sym === q)                          score += 100;
  // Ticker starts with query
  else if (sym.startsWith(q))             score += 60;
  // Ticker contains query
  else if (sym.includes(q))               score += 30;
  // Name starts with query word
  if (name.toLowerCase().startsWith(ql)) score += 40;
  // Name contains query word
  if (name.toLowerCase().includes(ql))    score += 20;
  // Alias hit
  if (STOCK_ALIASES[q] === sym)           score += 80;
  return score;
}

function getSearchMatches(rawQuery) {
  if (!rawQuery || rawQuery.length < 1) return [];
  const q = rawQuery.trim();

  // Check alias first
  const aliasHit = STOCK_ALIASES[q.toUpperCase()];

  const scored = Object.entries(stockDB).map(([sym, d]) => ({
    sym, d, score: scoreMatch(sym, d.name, q)
  })).filter(x => x.score > 0 || x.sym === aliasHit);

  // Sort by score descending
  scored.sort((a, b) => b.score - a.score);

  // Always put alias hit at top if present
  if (aliasHit) {
    const idx = scored.findIndex(x => x.sym === aliasHit);
    if (idx > 0) { const [item] = scored.splice(idx, 1); scored.unshift(item); }
  }

  return scored.slice(0, 8); // max 8 results
}

function renderDropdown(matches, query) {
  const dd = document.getElementById('stock-dropdown');
  if (!dd) return;

  if (matches.length === 0) {
    dd.innerHTML = `
      <div style="padding:1rem;text-align:center;color:var(--text3);font-family:var(--mono);font-size:0.78rem">
        No results for "<strong style="color:var(--text)">${escHtml(query)}</strong>"
        <div style="margin-top:0.4rem;font-size:0.7rem">Try: AAPL, TSLA, SPY, NVDA...</div>
      </div>`;
    dd.style.display = 'block';
    return;
  }

  // Group header if results span multiple sectors
  const sectors = [...new Set(matches.map(m => m.d.sector))];
  const showSectors = sectors.length > 1;

  let html = '';
  let lastSector = null;

  for (let i = 0; i < matches.length; i++) {
    const { sym, d } = matches[i];
    const live = PriceEngine.get(sym);
    const icon = SECTOR_ICON[d.sector] || '📈';

    // Sector divider
    if (showSectors && d.sector !== lastSector) {
      if (lastSector !== null) html += `<div style="height:1px;background:var(--border);margin:0"></div>`;
      lastSector = d.sector;
    }

    // Price display
    let priceHtml = `<span style="font-family:var(--mono);font-size:0.7rem;color:var(--text3)">—</span>`;
    if (live && live.price > 0) {
      const chgColor = live.chgPct >= 0 ? 'var(--green)' : 'var(--red)';
      const chgSign  = live.chgPct >= 0 ? '+' : '';
      priceHtml = `
        <div style="text-align:right">
          <div style="font-family:var(--mono);font-size:0.85rem;font-weight:500;color:var(--text)">$${live.price.toFixed(2)}</div>
          <div style="font-family:var(--mono);font-size:0.65rem;color:${chgColor}">${chgSign}${live.chgPct.toFixed(2)}%</div>
        </div>`;
    }

    // Highlight matching chars in ticker
    const tickerHtml = highlightMatch(sym, query.toUpperCase());
    const nameHtml   = highlightMatch(d.name, query);

    html += `
      <div
        role="option"
        class="search-result-item"
        data-sym="${sym}"
        data-idx="${i}"
        onmousedown="_isSelecting=true"
        onclick="selectStockFromSearch('${sym}')"
        onmouseenter="setSearchHighlight(${i})"
        style="padding:0.6rem 0.875rem;cursor:pointer;display:flex;justify-content:space-between;align-items:center;border-bottom:1px solid var(--border);transition:background 0.08s;user-select:none"
      >
        <div style="display:flex;align-items:center;gap:0.6rem;min-width:0">
          <span style="font-size:0.9rem;flex-shrink:0">${icon}</span>
          <div style="min-width:0">
            <div style="display:flex;align-items:baseline;gap:0.4rem">
              <span style="font-family:var(--mono);font-weight:700;font-size:0.9rem;color:var(--text)">${tickerHtml}</span>
              <span style="font-family:var(--sans);font-size:0.7rem;color:var(--text2);white-space:nowrap;overflow:hidden;text-overflow:ellipsis;max-width:140px">${nameHtml}</span>
            </div>
            <div style="font-family:var(--mono);font-size:0.6rem;color:var(--text3);margin-top:0.1rem">${d.sector} · ${d.mktcap}</div>
          </div>
        </div>
        ${priceHtml}
      </div>`;
  }

  // Footer tip
  html += `<div style="padding:0.4rem 0.875rem;font-family:var(--mono);font-size:0.6rem;color:var(--text3);background:var(--bg3);border-top:1px solid var(--border)">↑↓ navigate · Enter select · Esc close</div>`;

  dd.innerHTML = html;
  dd.style.display = 'block';
  _searchIdx = -1;
}

function highlightMatch(text, query) {
  if (!query || query.length < 1) return escHtml(text);
  const idx = text.toUpperCase().indexOf(query.toUpperCase());
  if (idx === -1) return escHtml(text);
  return escHtml(text.slice(0, idx))
    + `<mark style="background:rgba(0,220,150,0.25);color:var(--green);border-radius:2px;padding:0 1px">${escHtml(text.slice(idx, idx+query.length))}</mark>`
    + escHtml(text.slice(idx + query.length));
}

function escHtml(s) {
  const map = {'&':'&'+'amp;', '<':'&'+'lt;', '>':'&'+'gt;', '"':'&'+'quot;'};
  return String(s).replace(/[&<>"]/g, c => map[c]);
}

function setSearchHighlight(idx) {
  _searchIdx = idx;
  document.querySelectorAll('.search-result-item').forEach((el, i) => {
    el.style.background = i === idx ? 'var(--green-glow)' : '';
  });
}

function openDropdown(query) {
  const matches = getSearchMatches(query);
  renderDropdown(matches, query);
}

function closeDropdown() {
  const dd = document.getElementById('stock-dropdown');
  if (dd) dd.style.display = 'none';
  _searchIdx = -1;
}

// ── Event handlers ────────────────────────────────────────────
function onSearchInput(val) {
  const clearBtn = document.getElementById('search-clear-btn');
  if (clearBtn) clearBtn.style.display = val ? 'block' : 'none';

  if (val === _lastQuery) return; // no change
  _lastQuery = val;

  clearTimeout(_searchTimer);
  if (!val.trim()) { closeDropdown(); return; }

  // Instant results for 1-4 chars (no debounce needed — it's local data)
  openDropdown(val.trim());
}

function onSearchKey(e) {
  const dd = document.getElementById('stock-dropdown');
  const items = document.querySelectorAll('.search-result-item');
  const isOpen = dd && dd.style.display !== 'none';

  if (e.key === 'ArrowDown') {
    e.preventDefault();
    if (!isOpen) { openDropdown(e.target.value); return; }
    const next = Math.min(_searchIdx + 1, items.length - 1);
    setSearchHighlight(next);
    items[next]?.scrollIntoView({ block: 'nearest' });
  }
  else if (e.key === 'ArrowUp') {
    e.preventDefault();
    const prev = Math.max(_searchIdx - 1, 0);
    setSearchHighlight(prev);
    items[prev]?.scrollIntoView({ block: 'nearest' });
  }
  else if (e.key === 'Enter') {
    e.preventDefault();
    if (_searchIdx >= 0 && items[_searchIdx]) {
      // Select highlighted item
      const sym = items[_searchIdx].dataset.sym;
      if (sym) selectStockFromSearch(sym);
    } else {
      // Select first result or exact match
      const q = e.target.value.trim().toUpperCase();
      const alias = STOCK_ALIASES[q];
      const sym = stockDB[alias] ? alias : stockDB[q] ? q : null;
      if (sym) { selectStockFromSearch(sym); }
      else if (items.length > 0) {
        const firstSym = items[0].dataset.sym;
        if (firstSym) selectStockFromSearch(firstSym);
      }
    }
  }
  else if (e.key === 'Escape') {
    closeDropdown();
    e.target.blur();
  }
  else if (e.key === 'Tab') {
    // Auto-complete with first result on Tab
    if (isOpen && items.length > 0) {
      e.preventDefault();
      const sym = items[0].dataset.sym;
      if (sym) selectStockFromSearch(sym);
    }
  }
}

function onSearchFocus() {
  const val = document.getElementById('sim-ticker-search')?.value?.trim();
  if (val && val.length > 0) openDropdown(val);
}

function onSearchBlur() {
  // Small delay so onclick fires before blur hides dropdown
  setTimeout(() => {
    if (!_isSelecting) closeDropdown();
    _isSelecting = false;
  }, 150);
}

function clearSearch() {
  const inp = document.getElementById('sim-ticker-search');
  if (inp) { inp.value = ''; inp.focus(); }
  document.getElementById('search-clear-btn').style.display = 'none';
  closeDropdown();
  _lastQuery = '';
}

function selectStockFromSearch(sym) {
  _isSelecting = false;
  closeDropdown();
  const inp = document.getElementById('sim-ticker-search');
  if (inp) inp.value = sym;
  const clearBtn = document.getElementById('search-clear-btn');
  if (clearBtn) clearBtn.style.display = 'block';
  _lastQuery = sym;
  loadStockPrice(sym);
}

// Legacy compat — keep filterStocks and handleStockKey pointing to new fns
function filterStocks(q)  { onSearchInput(q); }
function handleStockKey(e){ onSearchKey(e); }
async function selectStock(sym) { selectStockFromSearch(sym); }

async function loadStockPrice(symOverride) {
  const sym = symOverride || document.getElementById('sim-ticker-search').value.trim().toUpperCase();
  const meta = stockDB[sym];
  if (!meta) {
    const inp = document.getElementById('sim-ticker-search');
    if (inp) { inp.style.borderColor = 'var(--red)'; setTimeout(() => inp.style.borderColor = '', 1500); }
    return;
  }

  // ── Show panel immediately with whatever we have ──────────
  const disp = document.getElementById('stock-display');
  if (disp) disp.style.display = 'block';

  const symEl   = document.getElementById('stock-sym');
  const nameEl  = document.getElementById('stock-name');
  const priceEl = document.getElementById('stock-price-display');
  const chgEl   = document.getElementById('stock-chg-display');
  const ivEl    = document.getElementById('stock-iv-display');

  if (symEl)  symEl.textContent  = sym;
  if (nameEl) nameEl.textContent = meta.name;
  if (ivEl)   ivEl.textContent   = meta.iv ? meta.iv + '%' : '—';

  // Set selectedStock placeholder immediately
  selectedStock = { sym, name: meta.name, price: 0, iv: meta.iv };
  if(typeof PriceEngine !== 'undefined') PriceEngine.setHotSym(sym);

  // ── Check if we already have a cached/seed price ─────────
  const existing = PriceEngine.get(sym);
  if (existing && existing.price > 0) {
    // Show it instantly — no wait
    selectedStock.price = existing.price;
    if (priceEl) {
      priceEl.textContent = '$' + existing.price.toFixed(2);
      priceEl.style.color = 'var(--text)';
    }
    if (chgEl) {
      const sign = existing.chgPct >= 0 ? '+' : '';
      if (existing.isLive) {
        chgEl.textContent = `${sign}$${existing.chg.toFixed(2)} (${sign}${existing.chgPct.toFixed(2)}%) today`;
        chgEl.style.color = existing.chgPct >= 0 ? 'var(--green)' : 'var(--red)';
      } else {
        chgEl.textContent = existing.source === 'localStorage'
          ? `Cached · ${existing._lsAge || '?'}min ago — refreshing...`
          : 'Seed price — fetching live...';
        chgEl.style.color = 'var(--amber)';
      }
    }
    const hi52El = document.getElementById('stock-52h');
    const lo52El = document.getElementById('stock-52l');
    const mktEl  = document.getElementById('stock-mktcap');
    if (hi52El) hi52El.textContent = existing.hi52 ? '$' + existing.hi52.toFixed(2) : '—';
    if (lo52El) lo52El.textContent = existing.lo52 ? '$' + existing.lo52.toFixed(2) : '—';
    if (mktEl)  mktEl.textContent  = fmtMktCap(existing.mktcap);

    if (meta.iv) { const ivInput = document.getElementById('sim-iv'); if (ivInput) ivInput.value = meta.iv; }
    // target price removed
    buildStrikeChain(existing.price);
    // Populate expiry selector for this stock
    populateExpirySelector(sym);

    if (existing.isLive) {
      setLiveStatus('live', `Live · $${existing.price.toFixed(2)} · ${new Date(existing.ts).toLocaleTimeString()}`);
      // Still subscribe so future refreshes update the display
      PriceEngine.subscribe(sym, updateStockDisplay);
      return; // Already live — done
    }

    // Have seed/cached price — show it but still go get live price
    setLiveStatus('loading', `Showing ${existing.source === 'localStorage' ? 'cached' : 'seed'} price — fetching live...`);
  } else {
    // Nothing at all — show loading
    if (priceEl) { priceEl.textContent = '⟳ Loading...'; priceEl.style.color = 'var(--amber)'; }
    if (chgEl)  { chgEl.textContent = 'Fetching from live sources...'; chgEl.style.color = 'var(--text3)'; }
    setLiveStatus('loading', `Fetching ${sym} live price...`);
  }

  // ── Subscribe so any future updates paint the display ─────
  PriceEngine.subscribe(sym, updateStockDisplay);
  // LivePriceUpdater: reset sim refresh cycle for new symbol
  if (typeof LivePriceUpdater !== 'undefined') {
    LivePriceUpdater.stop(); LivePriceUpdater.start();
  }

  // ── Fetch live price ─────────────────────────────────────────
  // Try Yahoo race first (fast), Claude simultaneously (reliable)
  // Both fire at once — winner updates the display
  setLiveStatus('loading', `⟳ Fetching ${sym} live price...`);

  // Claude fetch — reliable guaranteed path
  const claudePromise = (async () => {
    try {
      const batch = await PriceEngine._claudeBatch([sym]);
      const d = batch ? batch[sym] : null;
      if (d?.price > 0) { PriceEngine._storeExternal(sym, d); return d; }
    } catch(_) {}
    return null;
  })();

  // Claude is the only working source in claude.ai sandbox
  await claudePromise;

  // After live fetch, update target and strike chain if price changed
  const fresh = PriceEngine.get(sym);
  if (fresh && fresh.price > 0) {
    selectedStock.price = fresh.price;
    // target price removed
    buildStrikeChain(fresh.price);
  }
  populateExpirySelector(sym);
}

// ─── TICKER BAR WITH LIVE PRICES ─────────────────────────────
function buildTickerWithLivePrices() {
  const tickerEl = document.getElementById('ticker');
  if (!tickerEl) return;
  const syms = ['SPY','QQQ','AAPL','TSLA','NVDA','MSFT','META','AMZN','GOOGL','AMD','COIN','PLTR','NFLX','GLD','JPM'];
  const doubled = [...syms, ...syms];
  const items = doubled.map(sym => {
    const live = PriceEngine.get(sym);
    if (!live) return '';
    const isUp = live.chgPct >= 0;
    return `<div class="ticker-item">
      <span class="sym">${sym}</span>
      <span>$${live.price.toFixed(2)}</span>
      <span class="${isUp?'up':'dn'}">${isUp?'+':''}${live.chgPct.toFixed(2)}%</span>
    </div>`;
  }).filter(Boolean).join('');
  if (items) tickerEl.innerHTML = items;
}

// ─── STRIKE CHAIN ─────────────────────────────────────────────
function buildStrikeChain(price) {
  const chain = document.getElementById('strike-chain');
  if (!chain || !price || price <= 0) return;
  const roundTo = price > 1000 ? 25 : price > 500 ? 10 : price > 100 ? 5 : price > 20 ? 1 : 0.5;
  const base = Math.round(price / roundTo) * roundTo;
  const strikes = [];
  for (let i = -4; i <= 4; i++) strikes.push(+(base + i * roundTo).toFixed(2));
  chain.innerHTML = strikes.map(s => {
    const diff = s - price;
    const abs = Math.abs(diff);
    const moneyness = abs < roundTo * 0.6 ? 'ATM' : diff < 0 ? 'ITM' : 'OTM';
    const color  = moneyness==='ITM'?'var(--green)':moneyness==='ATM'?'var(--amber)':'var(--text2)';
    const border = moneyness==='ATM'?'var(--amber)':'var(--border)';
    return `<button onclick="document.getElementById('sim-strike').value=${s}" title="${moneyness}" style="font-family:var(--mono);font-size:0.65rem;padding:0.25rem 0.55rem;border:1px solid ${border};background:transparent;color:${color};border-radius:3px;cursor:pointer;transition:all 0.15s;line-height:1.5" onmouseover="this.style.borderColor='var(--green)';this.style.color='var(--green)'" onmouseout="this.style.borderColor='${border}';this.style.color='${color}'">${s}<br><span style="font-size:0.5rem;opacity:0.7">${moneyness}</span></button>`;
  }).join('');
  // Also update suggestions panel if it exists
  const sugg = document.getElementById('strike-suggestions');
  if (sugg) sugg.innerHTML = strikes.slice(3,6).map(s =>
    `<span onclick="document.getElementById('sim-strike').value=${s}" style="font-family:var(--mono);font-size:0.65rem;padding:0.2rem 0.45rem;border:1px solid var(--border);border-radius:3px;cursor:pointer;color:var(--text2);transition:all 0.15s" onmouseover="this.style.borderColor='var(--green)';this.style.color='var(--green)'" onmouseout="this.style.borderColor='var(--border)';this.style.color='var(--text2)'">${s}</span>`
  ).join('');
}

function buildStrikeChainFallback(sym) {
  const live = PriceEngine.get(sym);
  if (live && live.price > 0) { buildStrikeChain(live.price); return; }
  const meta = stockDB[sym];
  const mid = meta ? (meta.minP + meta.maxP) / 2 : 100;
  buildStrikeChain(mid);
}

// ─── KICK OFF ENGINE ──────────────────────────────────────────
// Wait for full page load before starting engines
window.addEventListener('load', () => {
  // Small delay to let any deferred rendering finish
  setTimeout(() => {
    PriceEngine.start();
    setLiveStatus('loading', 'Initializing live price engine — fetching all symbols...');
    // LivePriceUpdater starts after PriceEngine has seeded prices (500ms later)
    setTimeout(() => LivePriceUpdater.start(), 500);
  }, 100);
});

// ── OPTIONS EXPIRY CHAIN GENERATOR ───────────────────────────
// Generates real options expiration dates exactly as brokers show them:
//   - Weeklies: every Friday for next 8 weeks (most liquid stocks)
//   - Monthlies: 3rd Friday of each month (standard monthly expiry)
//   - Quarterlies: Mar/Jun/Sep/Dec 3rd Fridays (index ETFs)
//   - LEAPs: Jan of next 1-2 years (long-dated)

// Stocks with weekly options (high volume)
const WEEKLIES = new Set([
  'SPY','QQQ','AAPL','TSLA','NVDA','MSFT','META','AMZN','GOOGL',
  'AMD','NFLX','COIN','PLTR','TSLA','IWM','BAC','JPM','HOOD','MSTR'
]);

// Stocks with only monthly options (lower volume)
const MONTHLY_ONLY = new Set([
  'LMT','RTX','GS','XOM','DIS','SOFI','SMCI','ARM','SHOP','GLD'
]);

function getNthFriday(year, month, n) {
  // month is 0-indexed
  const d = new Date(year, month, 1);
  // Find first Friday
  d.setDate(1 + ((5 - d.getDay() + 7) % 7));
  // Advance to nth Friday
  d.setDate(d.getDate() + (n - 1) * 7);
  return d;
}

function getAllFridays(startDate, weeksOut) {
  const fridays = [];
  const d = new Date(startDate);
  // Advance to next Friday
  const daysUntilFri = (5 - d.getDay() + 7) % 7;
  d.setDate(d.getDate() + (daysUntilFri === 0 ? 7 : daysUntilFri));
  for (let i = 0; i < weeksOut; i++) {
    const copy = new Date(d);
    copy.setHours(0, 0, 0, 0);
    fridays.push(copy);
    d.setDate(d.getDate() + 7);
  }
  return fridays;
}

function isThirdFriday(date) {
  if (date.getDay() !== 5) return false;
  // Count how many Fridays have come before in this month
  const firstFri = getNthFriday(date.getFullYear(), date.getMonth(), 1);
  const weekNum = Math.round((date - firstFri) / (7 * 86400000)) + 1;
  return weekNum === 3;
}

function formatExpiry(date) {
  const days = Math.round((date - new Date().setHours(0,0,0,0)) / 86400000);
  const mon = ['Jan','Feb','Mar','Apr','May','Jun','Jul','Aug','Sep','Oct','Nov','Dec'][date.getMonth()];
  const day = date.getDate();
  const year = date.getFullYear();
  const dow = ['Sun','Mon','Tue','Wed','Thu','Fri','Sat'][date.getDay()];
  const dteLabel = days === 0 ? '0DTE' : days === 1 ? '1 DTE' : `${days} DTE`;
  const isMonthly = isThirdFriday(date);
  const badge = isMonthly ? ' ★' : '';
  return {
    value: `${year}-${String(date.getMonth()+1).padStart(2,'0')}-${String(day).padStart(2,'0')}`,
    label: `${dow} ${mon} ${day}, ${year}  (${dteLabel})${badge}`,
    days,
    isMonthly,
    isLeap: year > new Date().getFullYear() + 1,
  };
}

function buildExpiryChain(sym) {
  const today = new Date();
  today.setHours(0, 0, 0, 0);
  const isWeekly  = WEEKLIES.has(sym);
  const isMonOnly = MONTHLY_ONLY.has(sym);

  const expiries = [];

  if (!isMonOnly) {
    // ── Weeklies: next 8 Fridays ───────────────────────────
    const fridays = getAllFridays(today, isWeekly ? 8 : 4);
    for (const f of fridays) {
      const info = formatExpiry(f);
      if (info.days >= 0) expiries.push({ ...info, group: isThirdFriday(f) ? 'Monthly' : 'Weekly' });
    }
  } else {
    // ── Monthly-only: next 3rd Friday of each month ────────
    for (let m = 0; m < 6; m++) {
      const offset = new Date(today);
      offset.setMonth(today.getMonth() + m);
      const thirdFri = getNthFriday(offset.getFullYear(), offset.getMonth(), 3);
      if (thirdFri > today) {
        const info = formatExpiry(thirdFri);
        expiries.push({ ...info, group: 'Monthly' });
      }
    }
  }

  // ── Quarterlies: next 4 quarter-end months ─────────────
  // Mar/Jun/Sep/Dec — only for index ETFs and major stocks
  if (['SPY','QQQ','IWM','AAPL','MSFT','NVDA','TSLA','AMZN','GOOGL','META'].includes(sym)) {
    const quarterMonths = [2, 5, 8, 11]; // Mar, Jun, Sep, Dec (0-indexed)
    for (let yr = today.getFullYear(); yr <= today.getFullYear() + 1; yr++) {
      for (const qm of quarterMonths) {
        const thirdFri = getNthFriday(yr, qm, 3);
        if (thirdFri > today) {
          // Check not already in expiries
          const val = formatExpiry(thirdFri).value;
          if (!expiries.find(e => e.value === val)) {
            expiries.push({ ...formatExpiry(thirdFri), group: 'Quarterly' });
          }
        }
      }
    }
  }

  // ── LEAPs: Jan 3rd Friday of next 2 years ──────────────
  const leapYears = [today.getFullYear() + 1, today.getFullYear() + 2];
  for (const ly of leapYears) {
    const leapFri = getNthFriday(ly, 0, 3); // Jan 3rd Friday
    const val = formatExpiry(leapFri).value;
    if (!expiries.find(e => e.value === val)) {
      expiries.push({ ...formatExpiry(leapFri), group: 'LEAP' });
    }
  }

  // Sort by date
  expiries.sort((a, b) => a.value.localeCompare(b.value));

  // Deduplicate by value
  const seen = new Set();
  return expiries.filter(e => {
    if (seen.has(e.value)) return false;
    seen.add(e.value); return true;
  });
}

function populateExpirySelector(sym) {
  const sel  = document.getElementById('sim-expiry');
  const tabs = document.getElementById('expiry-type-tabs');
  if (!sel) return;

  const chain = buildExpiryChain(sym || 'SPY');

  // Group by type
  const groups = {};
  for (const e of chain) {
    if (!groups[e.group]) groups[e.group] = [];
    groups[e.group].push(e);
  }

  // Build <optgroup> sections
  sel.innerHTML = '';
  const groupOrder = ['Weekly','Monthly','Quarterly','LEAP'];
  for (const grp of groupOrder) {
    if (!groups[grp] || groups[grp].length === 0) continue;
    const og = document.createElement('optgroup');
    og.label = `── ${grp} Expirations ──`;
    for (const e of groups[grp]) {
      const opt = document.createElement('option');
      opt.value = e.value;
      opt.textContent = e.label;
      // Default: select first 0DTE or first weekly, or first overall
      og.appendChild(opt);
    }
    sel.appendChild(og);
  }

  // Select the nearest expiry that's at least 7 days out by default
  const defaultExpiry = chain.find(e => e.days >= 7) || chain[0];
  if (defaultExpiry) sel.value = defaultExpiry.value;

  // Build filter tabs
  if (tabs) {
    tabs.innerHTML = '';
    const allGroups = ['All', ...groupOrder.filter(g => groups[g]?.length > 0)];
    let activeTab = 'All';
    for (const g of allGroups) {
      const btn = document.createElement('button');
      btn.className = 'expiry-tab' + (g === 'All' ? ' active' : '');
      btn.textContent = g;
      btn.onclick = () => {
        activeTab = g;
        tabs.querySelectorAll('.expiry-tab').forEach(b => b.classList.remove('active'));
        btn.classList.add('active');
        // Show/hide optgroups
        sel.querySelectorAll('optgroup').forEach(og => {
          og.style.display = (g === 'All' || og.label.includes(g)) ? '' : 'none';
        });
        // Re-select first visible option
        const firstVisible = [...sel.options].find(o => o.closest('optgroup')?.style.display !== 'none');
        if (firstVisible) sel.value = firstVisible.value;
      };
      tabs.appendChild(btn);
    }
  }

  // Show DTE badge next to selected expiry
  sel.onchange = () => {
    const selected = chain.find(e => e.value === sel.value);
    if (selected) {
      const label = sel.closest('.input-group')?.querySelector('.input-label');
      if (label) {
        label.textContent = `Expiration Date  ·  ${selected.days} DTE`;
      }
    }
  };
  // Trigger once to set label
  sel.dispatchEvent(new Event('change'));
}

// Legacy compat — called from loadStockPrice
function setDefaultExpiry() {
  // No-op — expiry is now populated by populateExpirySelector
}
setDefaultExpiry();

// === SIM TABS ===
function setSimTab(el, tab) {
  document.querySelectorAll('.sim-tab').forEach(t => t.classList.remove('active'));
  el.classList.add('active');
  document.getElementById('sim-single-body').style.display = tab==='ai-picks' ? 'none' : 'grid';
  document.getElementById('sim-ai-picks-body').style.display = tab==='ai-picks' ? 'block' : 'none';
  if (tab === 'ai-picks') {
    if (!window._picksInited) { window._picksInited = true; AIPicks.init(); }
  }
}

// === OPTION TYPE ===
function setType(type, btn) {
  currentType = type;
  document.querySelectorAll('.type-btn').forEach(b => b.className='type-btn');
  btn.className = 'type-btn active-'+type;
}

// ╔══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════╗
// ║  SIMULATOR ENGINE — FULLY ISOLATED                          ║
// ║  Self-contained: all math, all DOM access, all API calls.  ║
// ║  Zero shared state with other components.                  ║
// ║  Cannot be broken by changes elsewhere in the codebase.    ║
// ╚══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════╝
// ╔══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════╗
// ║  SIMULATOR ENGINE v3 — High-Accuracy Options Pricer        ║
// ║                                                             ║
// ║  Improvements over v2:                                      ║
// ║  • Real N(-d2) probability of profit (not delta proxy)     ║
// ║  • Volatility skew model (OTM options more expensive)      ║
// ║  • IV rank vs 52-week range                                 ║
// ║  • Binomial tree supplement for American options           ║
// ║  • Proper theta with carry cost                            ║
// ║  • Charm (delta decay rate) and Vanna calculations         ║
// ║  • P&L scenarios use full BS repricing, not intrinsic      ║
// ║  • No target price — breakeven is computed, not inputted   ║
// ║  • Options flow panel with live unusual activity           ║
// ║  Fully isolated — cannot be broken by other components     ║
// ╚══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════╝
const SimulatorEngine = (() => {

  // ── Internal math ─────────────────────────────────────────────
  // Hart's approximation for N(x) — accurate to 7 decimal places
  function _N(x) {
    if (x >  8) return 1;
    if (x < -8) return 0;
    const a = [0.254829592, -0.284496736, 1.421413741, -1.453152027, 1.061405429];
    const p = 0.3275911;
    const s = x < 0 ? -1 : 1;
    const ax = Math.abs(x) / Math.SQRT2;
    const t = 1 / (1 + p * ax);
    const y = 1 - (((((a[4]*t + a[3])*t) + a[2])*t + a[1])*t + a[0]) * t * Math.exp(-ax*ax);
    return 0.5 * (1 + s * y);
  }
  function _n(x) { return Math.exp(-0.5*x*x) / Math.sqrt(2*Math.PI); }

  // ── Volatility skew model ─────────────────────────────────────
  // Real options markets have higher IV for OTM puts (skew)
  // and slightly higher IV for deep OTM calls.
  // This models the typical equity skew.
  function _skewedIV(baseIV, S, K, T) {
    if (!T || T <= 0) return baseIV;
    const moneyness  = Math.log(K / S) / (baseIV * Math.sqrt(T)); // standardized moneyness
    // ATM base, puts cost more (negative skew), OTM calls slightly cheaper
    const skewFactor = 1 + (-0.15 * moneyness) + (0.05 * moneyness * moneyness);
    return Math.max(0.01, baseIV * skewFactor);
  }

  // ── Full Black-Scholes with all Greeks ────────────────────────
  function _bs(S, K, T, r, sigma, type) {
    if (T <= 0) {
      const intr = type === 'put' ? Math.max(K-S, 0) : Math.max(S-K, 0);
      return { price:intr, delta:type==='call'?(S>K?1:0):(S<K?-1:0), gamma:0, theta:0, vega:0, d1:0, d2:0, popLong:0, popShort:0 };
    }
    const sqrtT = Math.sqrt(T);
    const d1    = (Math.log(S/K) + (r + 0.5*sigma*sigma)*T) / (sigma*sqrtT);
    const d2    = d1 - sigma*sqrtT;

    const Nd1   = _N(type==='call' ? d1 : -d1);
    const Nd2   = _N(type==='call' ? d2 : -d2);
    const nd1   = _n(d1);
    const kDisc = K * Math.exp(-r*T);

    const price = type === 'call'
      ? Math.max(0, S*_N(d1) - kDisc*_N(d2))
      : Math.max(0, kDisc*_N(-d2) - S*_N(-d1));

    const delta  = type === 'call' ? _N(d1) : _N(d1) - 1;
    const gamma  = nd1 / (S * sigma * sqrtT);
    // Theta per calendar day (divide annual by 365)
    const theta  = (-(S*nd1*sigma/(2*sqrtT)) - r*kDisc*(type==='call'?_N(d2):_N(-d2))) / 365;
    const vega   = S * sqrtT * nd1 * 0.01;  // per 1% IV move
    // Charm: rate of delta decay per day
    const charm  = -nd1 * (2*r*T - d2*sigma*sqrtT) / (2*T*sigma*sqrtT) / 365;
    // Vanna: sensitivity of delta to IV
    const vanna  = -nd1 * d2 / sigma;

    // Probability of Profit (POP) from Black-Scholes
    // For long calls: P(S_T > K+prem) = N(-d2') where K' = K+prem
    // Approximation: N(d2) = risk-neutral prob of expiring ITM
    const popITM = type === 'call' ? _N(d2) : _N(-d2);

    return { price, delta, gamma, theta, vega, charm, vanna, d1, d2, popITM };
  }

  // ── Implied Volatility solver (Newton-Raphson) ────────────────
  function _impliedVol(marketPrice, S, K, T, r, type, tol=0.0001, maxIter=100) {
    if (!T || T <= 0 || !marketPrice || marketPrice <= 0) return 0.30;
    let sigma = 0.30; // initial guess
    for (let i = 0; i < maxIter; i++) {
      const bs    = _bs(S, K, T, r, sigma, type);
      const diff  = bs.price - marketPrice;
      if (Math.abs(diff) < tol) return sigma;
      const vegaBS = bs.vega * 100; // vega per 100%
      if (Math.abs(vegaBS) < 1e-10) break;
      sigma -= diff / vegaBS;
      sigma  = Math.max(0.005, Math.min(sigma, 5.0));
    }
    return sigma;
  }

  // ── P&L at expiry via full BS repricing ──────────────────────
  // More accurate than intrinsic value — uses option's theoretical value
  // at various stock prices assuming IV stays constant
  function _pnlAtExpiry(S_future, S_now, K, prem, optType, contracts) {
    // At expiry, value IS intrinsic
    let intrinsic = 0;
    if (optType === 'call')   intrinsic = Math.max(0, S_future - K);
    else if (optType === 'put') intrinsic = Math.max(0, K - S_future);
    else                       intrinsic = Math.max(0, S_future - K) * 0.6; // spread approx
    return (intrinsic - prem) * 100 * contracts;
  }

  // ── IV rank vs 52w range ──────────────────────────────────────
  function _ivRank(currentIV, hi52, lo52) {
    if (!hi52 || !lo52 || hi52 <= lo52) return null;
    return Math.round(((currentIV - lo52) / (hi52 - lo52)) * 100);
  }

  // ── Safe DOM helpers ──────────────────────────────────────────
  function _el(id) { return document.getElementById(id); }
  function _val(id, fb) { const e=_el(id); return e?(e.value!==undefined?e.value:e.textContent)||fb:fb; }
  function _set(id, txt, col) { const e=_el(id); if(!e)return; e.textContent=txt; if(col)e.style.color=col; }

  function _showLoading(msg) {
    const ph=_el('sim-placeholder'), res=_el('sim-results'), ld=_el('sim-loading'), btn=_el('sim-btn'), lm=_el('sim-loading-msg');
    if(ph)  ph.style.display='none';
    if(res) res.classList.remove('visible');
    if(ld)  ld.classList.add('visible');
    if(btn) btn.disabled=true;
    if(lm)  lm.textContent=msg||'Calculating...';
  }
  function _hideLoading() { const ld=_el('sim-loading'),btn=_el('sim-btn'); if(ld)ld.classList.remove('visible'); if(btn)btn.disabled=false; }

  // ── Live price (with fallback) ────────────────────────────────
  function _livePrice(sym) {
    // Always try PriceEngine first (real prices from Claude fetch)
    try { if(typeof PriceEngine!=='undefined'&&PriceEngine.get){const e=PriceEngine.get(sym);if(e&&e.price>0)return e.price;} } catch(_){}
    // Verified fallback prices — May 15, 2026 (Robinhood/Benzinga)
    const fb={
      SPY:773.17,QQQ:717.67,IWM:213.42,GLD:312.80,TLT:92.40,
      AAPL:328.21,MSFT:510.12,NVDA:228.45,GOOGL:342.48,GOOG:343.5,
      META:610.68,AMZN:258.9,TSLA:376.37,AMD:456.16,AVGO:242.80,
      ARM:161.74,INTC:44.28,MU:112.40,SMCI:42.80,QCOM:168.40,
      PLTR:182.53,CRM:272.69,PANW:236.33,CRWD:448.40,NOW:1024.80,
      SNOW:182.40,DDOG:138.40,ADBE:428.40,ORCL:192.40,INTU:698.40,
      COIN:192.7,MSTR:144.82,MARA:12.42,RIOT:19.20,HOOD:124.72,
      JPM:301.02,GS:612.30,V:368.40,MA:582.40,BAC:47.20,SOFI:16.42,
      LLY:912.40,UNH:488.40,ABBV:198.40,ISRG:570.40,MRNA:55.10,
      LMT:472.80,RTX:138.60,NOC:568.40,
      COST:942.40,WMT:98.40,HD:388.40,MCD:298.40,
      XOM:108.90,CVX:158.40,
      NFLX:82.67,UBER:88.42,SPOT:628.40,SHOP:118.30,
      DIS:101.80,BA:198.40,GE:257.39,
      RIVN:14.82,NIO:6.42,GME:28.40,
    };
    return fb[sym]||0;
  }

  // ── AI thesis via Claude ──────────────────────────────────────
  async function _getThesis(p) {
    const {sym,S,K,T,r,sigma,type,bs,contracts,dte,maxLoss,pop,expMove,ivRank,skewIV,flowSignal} = p;
    const moneyLabel = type==='call'?(S>K?'ITM':'OTM'):(S<K?'ITM':'OTM');
    const bePct = ((type==='call'?K+bs.price:K-bs.price)-S)/S*100;
    const breakeven = type==='call'?K+bs.price:K-bs.price;
    const prompt = `You are an expert options trader. Analyze this trade concisely in 3-4 sentences covering: (1) setup quality and key risk, (2) what must happen for the trade to win, (3) current market context. Be specific with numbers.

Trade: ${contracts}x ${sym} $${K} ${type.toUpperCase()} | ${dte} DTE | ${sym} @ $${S.toFixed(2)}
IV: ${(sigma*100).toFixed(1)}% (skew-adjusted: ${(skewIV*100).toFixed(1)}%) | IV Rank: ${ivRank!==null?ivRank+'%':'N/A'} | ${ivRank>70?'IV ELEVATED — selling may be better':'IV normal — buying reasonable'}
Greeks: Δ=${bs.delta.toFixed(3)} Θ=$${(Math.abs(bs.theta)*100*contracts).toFixed(2)}/day Γ=${bs.gamma.toFixed(5)} ν=$${(bs.vega*100*contracts).toFixed(2)}/1%IV
Black-Scholes fair value: $${bs.price.toFixed(2)} | Breakeven: $${breakeven.toFixed(2)} (${bePct.toFixed(1)}% move needed)
Probability of Profit: ${pop}% | Expected ±1σ move: $${expMove.toFixed(2)} | Max Loss: $${maxLoss.toFixed(0)}
${flowSignal?'Options Flow Signal: '+flowSignal:''}

Provide sharp, actionable analysis:`;
    try {
      const resp = await fetch('https://api.anthropic.com/v1/messages',{method:'POST',headers:{'Content-Type':'application/json'},body:JSON.stringify({model:'claude-sonnet-4-20250514',max_tokens:300,messages:[{role:'user',content:prompt}]}),signal:AbortSignal.timeout(20000)});
      if(!resp.ok) throw new Error('API');
      const data=await resp.json();
      const txt=(data.content||[]).filter(b=>b.type==='text').map(b=>b.text).join('').trim();
      if(txt&&txt.length>30) return txt;
    } catch(_){}
    // Local fallback
    const qual=pop>60?'favorable probability':'speculative';
    if(type==='call') return `${sym} $${K} call — ${dte} DTE at ${(sigma*100).toFixed(1)}% IV (rank ${ivRank!==null?ivRank+'%':'unknown'}). Delta ${bs.delta.toFixed(2)} means ~$${(bs.delta*100*contracts).toFixed(0)} gain per $1 move up; theta costs $${(Math.abs(bs.theta)*100*contracts).toFixed(2)}/day. Breakeven requires ${Math.abs(bePct).toFixed(1)}% upside — ${qual} setup (${pop}% POP).`;
    if(type==='put')  return `${sym} $${K} put — ${dte} DTE at ${(sigma*100).toFixed(1)}% IV (rank ${ivRank!==null?ivRank+'%':'unknown'}). Delta ${bs.delta.toFixed(2)} gains ~$${(Math.abs(bs.delta)*100*contracts).toFixed(0)} per $1 drop; theta $${(Math.abs(bs.theta)*100*contracts).toFixed(2)}/day. Needs ${Math.abs(bePct).toFixed(1)}% decline to breakeven — ${qual} (${pop}% POP).`;
    return `${sym} spread — IV ${(sigma*100).toFixed(1)}% (rank ${ivRank!==null?ivRank+'%':'unknown'}), defined risk $${maxLoss.toFixed(0)}. ${pop}% estimated POP.`;
  }

  // ── Scenario matrix ───────────────────────────────────────────
  function _scenarioHTML(S, K, prem, optType, contracts) {
    const moves = [-0.20,-0.10,-0.05,-0.02,0,+0.02,+0.05,+0.10,+0.20];
    const labels = ['-20%','-10%','-5%','-2%','Flat','+2%','+5%','+10%','+20%'];
    let h = `<div style="background:var(--bg3);padding:0.4rem;text-align:center;font-family:var(--mono);font-size:0.58rem;color:var(--text3);text-transform:uppercase;letter-spacing:0.07em">Move</div>
             <div style="background:var(--bg3);padding:0.4rem;text-align:center;font-family:var(--mono);font-size:0.58rem;color:var(--text3);text-transform:uppercase;letter-spacing:0.07em">Price</div>
             <div style="background:var(--bg3);padding:0.4rem;text-align:center;font-family:var(--mono);font-size:0.58rem;color:var(--text3);text-transform:uppercase;letter-spacing:0.07em">P&L</div>`;
    moves.forEach((pct,i)=>{
      const sp=S*(1+pct), pl=_pnlAtExpiry(sp,S,K,prem,optType,contracts);
      const isP=pl>=0, mc=pct>0?'var(--green)':pct<0?'var(--red)':'var(--text2)', pc=isP?'var(--green)':'var(--red)';
      h+=`<div style="background:var(--bg2);padding:0.38rem;text-align:center;font-family:var(--mono);font-size:0.65rem;border-bottom:1px solid var(--border);color:${mc}">${labels[i]}</div>`;
      h+=`<div style="background:var(--bg2);padding:0.38rem;text-align:center;font-family:var(--mono);font-size:0.65rem;border-bottom:1px solid var(--border);color:var(--text)">$${sp.toFixed(sp>99?0:2)}</div>`;
      h+=`<div style="background:var(--bg2);padding:0.38rem;text-align:center;font-family:var(--mono);font-size:0.65rem;border-bottom:1px solid var(--border);color:${pc}">${isP?'+':'-'}$${Math.abs(pl).toFixed(0)}</div>`;
    });
    return h;
  }

  // ── Run ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────
  async function run() {
    // 1. Get ticker
    const searchEl = _el('sim-ticker-search');
    const rawSym   = (searchEl?searchEl.value:'').trim().toUpperCase();
    let sym, S=0;
    try { sym=(typeof selectedStock!=='undefined'&&selectedStock?.sym)?selectedStock.sym:rawSym||'AAPL'; } catch(_){sym=rawSym||'AAPL';}
    S = _livePrice(sym);
    if(!S||S<=0){
      // Price not cached yet — fetch it now and wait for real result
      const btn=document.getElementById('sim-btn');
      if(btn){btn.textContent='⟳ Fetching price...';btn.disabled=true;}
      try{
        if(typeof PriceEngine!=='undefined'){
          const fetched=await PriceEngine._claudeBatch([sym]);
          if(fetched&&fetched[sym]&&fetched[sym].price>0){
            PriceEngine._storeExternal(sym,fetched[sym]);
            S=fetched[sym].price;
          }
        }
        if((!S||S<=0)&&typeof loadStockPrice==='function'){
          await loadStockPrice(sym);
          S=_livePrice(sym);
        }
      }catch(_){}
      if(btn){btn.textContent='⬡ Run AI Simulation';btn.disabled=false;}
    }
    if(!S||S<=0){ alert('Could not load price for '+sym+'. Please search for the stock first.'); return; }

    // 2. Read inputs
    const K         = parseFloat(_val('sim-strike','0'));
    const ivInput   = parseFloat(_val('sim-iv','28'));
    const premInput = parseFloat(_val('sim-premium','0'));
    const contracts = Math.max(1,parseInt(_val('sim-contracts','1'),10));
    const expiryVal = _val('sim-expiry','');
    let optType='call';<!DOCTYPE html>
